import { Link } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { LayersIcon } from "lucide-react";

import { ModeToggle } from "#src/components/mode-toggle.tsx";

const links = [{ to: "/", label: "Home" }] as const;

export const Header = () => (
  <header className="bg-background/80 sticky top-0 z-10 border-b backdrop-blur-lg">
    <div className="mx-auto flex h-14 max-w-5xl items-center gap-3 px-5 sm:gap-6 sm:px-8">
      <Link
        className="flex items-center gap-2.5 font-semibold tracking-tight"
        to="/"
      >
        <span
          aria-hidden="true"
          className="bg-primary text-primary-foreground grid size-7 place-items-center rounded-lg [&_svg]:size-4"
        >
          <LayersIcon />
        </span>
        <span className="sr-only sm:not-sr-only">my-app</span>
      </Link>
      <nav className="flex gap-1 text-sm">
        {links.map(({ to, label }) => (
          <Link
            activeOptions={{ exact: true }}
            activeProps={{ className: "bg-muted text-foreground" }}
            className="hover:text-foreground rounded-md px-2.5 py-1.5 font-medium transition-colors"
            inactiveProps={{ className: "text-muted-foreground" }}
            key={to}
            to={to}
          >
            {label}
          </Link>
        ))}
      </nav>
      <div className="ml-auto">
        <ModeToggle />
      </div>
    </div>
  </header>
);

  { to: "/", label: "Home" },
  { to: "/todos", label: "Todos" },
] as const;

  <header className="bg-background/80 sticky top-0 z-10 border-b backdrop-blur-lg">
    <div className="mx-auto flex h-14 max-w-5xl items-center gap-3 px-5 sm:gap-6 sm:px-8">
      <Link
        className="flex items-center gap-2.5 font-semibold tracking-tight"
        to="/"
      >
        <span
          aria-hidden="true"

          <LayersIcon />
        </span>
        <span className="sr-only sm:not-sr-only">my-app</span>
      </Link>
      <nav className="flex gap-1 text-sm">
        {links.map(({ to, label }) => (
          <Link
            activeOptions={{ exact: true }}
            activeProps={{ className: "bg-muted text-foreground" }}
            className="hover:text-foreground rounded-md px-2.5 py-1.5 font-medium transition-colors"
            inactiveProps={{ className: "text-muted-foreground" }}
            key={to}
            to={to}
          >
            {label}
          </Link>
        ))}
      </nav>

  </header>

import { Link } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { LayersIcon } from "lucide-react";

import { ModeToggle } from "#src/components/mode-toggle.tsx";

const links = [
  { to: "/", label: "Home" },
  { to: "/todos", label: "Todos" },
] as const;

export const Header = () => (
  <header className="bg-background/80 sticky top-0 z-10 border-b backdrop-blur-lg">
    <div className="mx-auto flex h-14 max-w-5xl items-center gap-3 px-5 sm:gap-6 sm:px-8">
      <Link
        className="flex items-center gap-2.5 font-semibold tracking-tight"
        to="/"
      >
        <span
          aria-hidden="true"
          className="bg-primary text-primary-foreground grid size-7 place-items-center rounded-lg [&_svg]:size-4"
        >
          <LayersIcon />
        </span>
        <span className="sr-only sm:not-sr-only">my-app</span>
      </Link>
      <nav className="flex gap-1 text-sm">
        {links.map(({ to, label }) => (
          <Link
            activeOptions={{ exact: true }}
            activeProps={{ className: "bg-muted text-foreground" }}
            className="hover:text-foreground rounded-md px-2.5 py-1.5 font-medium transition-colors"
            inactiveProps={{ className: "text-muted-foreground" }}
            key={to}
            to={to}
          >
            {label}
          </Link>
        ))}
      </nav>
      <div className="ml-auto">
        <ModeToggle />
      </div>
    </div>
  </header>
);

  <header className="bg-background/80 sticky top-0 z-10 border-b backdrop-blur-lg">
    <div className="mx-auto flex h-14 max-w-5xl items-center gap-3 px-5 sm:gap-6 sm:px-8">
      <Link
        className="flex items-center gap-2.5 font-semibold tracking-tight"
        to="/"
      >
        <span
          aria-hidden="true"
          className="bg-primary text-primary-foreground grid size-7 place-items-center rounded-lg [&_svg]:size-4"
        >
          <LayersIcon />
        </span>
        <span className="sr-only sm:not-sr-only">my-app</span>
      </Link>
      <nav className="flex gap-1 text-sm">
        {links.map(({ to, label }) => (
          <Link
            activeOptions={{ exact: true }}
            activeProps={{ className: "bg-muted text-foreground" }}
            className="hover:text-foreground rounded-md px-2.5 py-1.5 font-medium transition-colors"
            inactiveProps={{ className: "text-muted-foreground" }}
            key={to}
            to={to}
          >
            {label}
          </Link>
        ))}
      </nav>
      <div className="ml-auto flex items-center gap-1.5">
        <UserMenu />
        <ModeToggle />
      </div>
    </div>
  </header>
);

#   tanstack-start-workers

import { MonitorIcon, MoonIcon, SunIcon } from "lucide-react";
import { useTheme } from "next-themes";

import { Button } from "@my-app/ui/components/button";
import {
  DropdownMenu,
  DropdownMenuContent,
  DropdownMenuRadioGroup,
  DropdownMenuRadioItem,
  DropdownMenuTrigger,
} from "@my-app/ui/components/dropdown-menu";

import { useHydrated } from "#src/hooks/use-hydrated.ts";

const themes = [
  { icon: SunIcon, label: "Light", value: "light" },
  { icon: MoonIcon, label: "Dark", value: "dark" },
  { icon: MonitorIcon, label: "System", value: "system" },
] as const;

export const ModeToggle = () => {
  const hydrated = useHydrated();
  const { theme, setTheme } = useTheme();

  return (
    <DropdownMenu>
      <DropdownMenuTrigger
        disabled={!hydrated}
        render={
          <Button aria-label="Toggle theme" size="icon" variant="ghost" />
        }
      >
        <SunIcon className="dark:hidden" />
        <MoonIcon className="hidden dark:block" />
      </DropdownMenuTrigger>
      <DropdownMenuContent align="end">
        <DropdownMenuRadioGroup onValueChange={setTheme} value={theme}>
          {themes.map(({ icon: Icon, label, value }) => (
            <DropdownMenuRadioItem key={value} value={value}>
              <Icon />
              {label}
            </DropdownMenuRadioItem>
          ))}
        </DropdownMenuRadioGroup>
      </DropdownMenuContent>
    </DropdownMenu>
  );
};

#   tanstack-start-ui-sdk-workers
#   tanstack-start-ui-sdk-workers-e2e
#   tanstack-start-workers
#   tanstack-start-workers-e2e
import { MutationCache, QueryCache, QueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { toast } from "sonner";

export const createQueryClient = () => {
  const queryClient: QueryClient = new QueryClient({
    queryCache: new QueryCache({
      onError: (error) => {
        toast.error(error.message, {
          action: {
            label: "Retry",
            onClick: () => {
              void queryClient.invalidateQueries();
            },
          },
        });
      },
    }),
    mutationCache: new MutationCache({
      onError: (error) => {
        toast.error(error.message);
      },
    }),
  });

  return queryClient;
};

import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

import { resolveConfig } from "vite";

// Router and Start write route types in configResolved, before Vite bundles the app.
await resolveConfig(
  { root: fileURLToPath(new URL("../apps/web", import.meta.url)) },
  "build"
);
